          <p>(a) On May 1, 1996, and each year thereafter, the board of the Minnesota State Academies shall count the actual number of Minnesota resident special education eligible students enrolled and receiving education services at the Minnesota State Academy for the Deaf and the Minnesota State Academy for the Blind.</p>
          <p>(b) For fiscal year 2006, the board of the Minnesota State Academies shall deposit in the state treasury an amount equal to all tuition received for the basic revenue according to subdivision 3, less the amount calculated in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(c) For fiscal year 2006, the Minnesota State Academies shall credit to their general operation account an amount equal to the tuition received which represents tuition earned for the total number of students over 175 based on:</p>
          <p>(1) the total number of enrolled students on May 1 less 175; times</p>
          <p>(2) the ratio of the number of students in that grade category to the total number of students on May 1; times</p>
          <p>(3) the general education revenue formula allowance; times</p>
          <p>(4) the pupil unit weighting factor pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05">126C.05</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) For fiscal year 2007 and later, the Minnesota State Academies shall report to the department the number of students by grade level counted according to paragraph (a). The amount paid to the Minnesota State Academies under subdivision 3, paragraph (c), must be reduced by an amount equal to:</p>
          <p>(1) the ratio of 175 to the total number of students on May 1; times</p>
          <p>(2) the total basic revenue determined according to subdivision 3, paragraph (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.65.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Calculation.</span></h2>
          <p>The sum provided by the calculation in subdivision 8 must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to the general operation account of the Minnesota State Academy for the Deaf and the Minnesota State Academy for the Blind.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.65.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Annual appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>There is annually appropriated to the department for the Minnesota State Academies the tuition or aid payment amounts received and credited to the general operation account of the academies under this section. A balance in an appropriation under this paragraph does not cancel but is available in successive fiscal years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.65.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Third-party reimbursement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The Minnesota State Academies must seek reimbursement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.21">125A.21</a> from third parties for the cost of services provided by the Minnesota State Academies whenever the services provided are otherwise covered by a child's public or private health plan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.65.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.66">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.66 OBLIGATIONS OF THE ACADEMIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.66.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Various levels of service.</span></h2>
          <p>The academies must provide their pupils with the levels of service defined in rules of the state board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.66.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Pupils' developmental needs.</span></h2>
          <p>The academies must deal with the developmental needs of their pupils.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.66.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Education with pupils without a disability.</span></h2>
          <p>The academies must provide opportunities for their pupils to be educated with pupils without a disability. A pupil's opportunities must be consistent with the pupil's individualized education program or individual family service plan and assessment.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.66.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.3.38.0">1991 c 265 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.13.55.0">1993 c 224 art 13 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.11.12.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 11 s 12</a>; 1Sp<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/11/#laws.3.12.0">2011 c 11 art 3 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.67">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.67 STAFF OF THE ACADEMIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Academies' administrator.</span></h2>
          <p>The position of the chief administrator at each academy is in the unclassified service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Teacher standards.</span></h2>
          <p>A teacher at the academies is subject to the licensure standards of the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board. An administrator at the academies is subject to the licensure standards of the Board of School Administrators.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Instructional supervisors.</span></h2>
          <p>An instructional supervisor at the Academy for the Deaf or the Academy for the Blind must have appropriate postsecondary credits from a teacher education program for teachers of the deaf or teachers of the blind, respectively. An instructional supervisor must have experience working with pupils with a disability.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Signing skills.</span></h2>
          <p>A staff member at the Academy for the Deaf must have the sign language communication skills appropriate for the staff member's job.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Braille skills.</span></h2>
          <p>A staff member at the Academy for the Blind must be knowledgeable in Braille communication as appropriate for the staff member's job.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Permanent employees must sign or know Braille.</span></h2>
          <p>An employee hired after August 1, 1985, cannot get permanent status until the employee is proficient in sign language if employed at the Academy for the Deaf or knowledgeable in Braille if employed at the Academy for the Blind.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.67.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Workers' compensation for trainees.</span></h2>
          <p>Student teachers and student trainees are employees of the academies for workers' compensation coverage.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.67.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.3.38.0">1991 c 265 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.11.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 11 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.55.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.29.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 29</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.68">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.68 STATE ADOPTED PROCEDURES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.68.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Subjects.</span></h2>
          <p>The board of the Minnesota State Academies must establish procedures for:</p>
          <p>(1) admission, including short-term admission, to the academies;</p>
          <p>(2) discharge from the academies;</p>
          <p>(3) decisions on a pupil's program at the academies; and</p>
          <p>(4) evaluation of a pupil's progress at the academies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.68.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Minimum content.</span></h2>
          <p>The discharge procedures must include reasonable notice to the child's district of residence. The procedures must guarantee a pupil and the pupil's parent or guardian appropriate safeguards. The safeguards must include a review of the placement determination made under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.69">125A.69</a> and the right to participate in educational program decisions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.68.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Not contested case.</span></h2>
          <p>A proceeding about admission to or discharge from the academies or about a pupil's program or progress at the academies is not a contested case under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.02">14.02</a>. The proceeding is governed instead by the rules governing special education.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.68.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.11.15.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 11 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.25.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 25</a>,26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.28.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.69">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.69 ADMISSION STANDARDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.69.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Admissions.</span></h2>
          <p> Admission to the Minnesota State Academies is described in this section.</p>
          <p>(a) A pupil who is deaf, hard-of-hearing, or deafblind, may be admitted to the Academy for the Deaf. A pupil who is blind or visually impaired, deafblind, or multiply disabled may be admitted to the Academy for the Blind. For a pupil to be admitted, two decisions must be made under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>.</p>
          <p>(1) It must be decided by the individualized education program team that education in regular or special education classes in the pupil's district of residence cannot be achieved satisfactorily because of the nature and severity of the deafness or blindness or visual impairment respectively.</p>
          <p>(2) It must be decided by the individualized education program team that the academy provides the most appropriate placement within the least restrictive alternative for the pupil.</p>
          <p>(b) A deaf or hard-of-hearing child or a visually impaired pupil may be admitted to get socialization skills or on a short-term basis for skills development.</p>
          <p>(c) A parent of a child who resides in Minnesota and who meets the disability criteria for being deaf or hard-of-hearing, blind or visually impaired, or multiply disabled may apply to place the child in the Minnesota State Academies. Academy staff must review the application to determine whether the Minnesota State Academies is an appropriate placement for the child. If academy staff determine that the Minnesota State Academies is an appropriate placement, the staff must invite the individualized education program team at the child's resident school district to participate in a meeting to arrange a trial placement of between 60 and 90 calendar days at the Minnesota State Academies. If the child's parent consents to the trial placement, the Minnesota State Academies is the responsible serving school district and incurs all due process obligations under law, and the child's resident school district is responsible for any transportation included in the child's individualized education program during the trial placement. Before the trial placement ends, academy staff must convene an individualized education program team meeting to determine whether to continue the child's placement at the Minnesota State Academies or that another placement is appropriate. If the academy members of the individualized education program team and the parent are unable to agree on the child's placement, the child's placement reverts to the placement in the child's individualized education program that immediately preceded the trial placement. If the parent and individualized education program team agree to continue the placement beyond the trial period, the transportation and due process responsibilities are the same as those described for the trial placement under this paragraph.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.69.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Pupils with multiple disabilities eligible to attend.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not prevent a pupil with disabilities in addition to being</p>
          <p>(1) deaf or hard-of-hearing, or</p>
          <p>(2) blind or visually impaired</p>
          <p class="bl">from attending the Academy for the Deaf or the Academy for the Blind, respectively.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.69.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Out-of-state admissions.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant from another state who can benefit from attending either academy may be admitted to the academy if the admission does not prevent an eligible Minnesota resident from being admitted. The board of the Minnesota State Academies must obtain reimbursement from the other state for the costs of the out-of-state admission. The state board may enter into an agreement with the appropriate authority in the other state for the reimbursement. Money received from another state must be deposited in the special revenue fund and credited to the general operating account of the academies. The money is appropriated to the academies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.69.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Compulsory attendance.</span></h2>
          <p>The compulsory attendance provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22">120A.22</a> apply to attendance at the academies. Attendance may be excused under that section by the commissioner of education or a designee. A person who fails to comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22">120A.22</a> is subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.26">120A.26</a>. The academies' administrator must exercise the duties imposed on a superintendent by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.26">120A.26</a>. Attendance at the Academy for the Deaf or the Academy for the Blind fulfills the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>. The academies are subject to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.40">121A.40</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/121A.45">121A.45</a>, the Pupil Fair Dismissal Act of 1970, as amended.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.69.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.70.0">1976 c 271 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/240/#laws.0.5.0">1985 c 240 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.55.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.11.12.0">1991 c 265 art 11 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.11.16.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 11 s 16</a>,17; art 16 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.29.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 29</a>,30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">2005 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/263/#laws.5.6.0">2006 c 263 art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.3.10.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 3 s 10</a>,12</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.70">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.70 EXPENSE OF PUPILS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.70.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Person liable for pupil's support pays expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The person who is liable for the support of a pupil attending a state academy must provide the pupil with enough money for proper clothing, postage, and necessary incidental expenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.70.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Local social services agency.</span></h2>
          <p>If the person liable for support of a pupil cannot support the pupil, the local social services agency of the county of the pupil's residence must do so. The commissioner of education must decide how much the local social services agency must pay. The board of the Minnesota State Academies must adopt rules that tell how the commissioner is to fix the amount. The local social services agency must make the payment to the superintendent of the school district of residence.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.70.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Transportation expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The school district of residence must pay for the transportation of the pupil from the pupil's residence to the academy and back.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.70.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/271/#laws.0.72.0">1976 c 271 s 72</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/449/#laws.0.15.0">1977 c 449 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.55.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/631/#laws.0.31.0">1994 c 631 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.27.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.31.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.71">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.71 DEPOSIT AND APPROPRIATION OF RENTS AND FEES OF ACADEMIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.71.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Rental income; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>Rental income must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to a revolving fund of the academies. Money in the revolving fund for rental income is annually appropriated to the academies for staff development purposes. Payment from the revolving fund for rental income may be made only according to vouchers authorized by the administrator of the academies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.71.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fees; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>Income received under subdivision 3 must be deposited in the state treasury and credited to a revolving fund of the academies. Money in the revolving fund for fees from conferences, seminars, technical assistance, production of instructionally related materials and other services is annually appropriated to the academies to defray expenses of those services. Payment from the revolving fund for conferences and other fees may be made only according to vouchers authorized by the administrator of the academies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.71.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contracts; fees; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>The board of the Minnesota State Academies may enter into agreements for the academies to provide respite care and supplemental educational instruction and services including assessments and counseling. The agreements may be made with public or private agencies or institutions, school districts, service cooperatives, or counties. The board may authorize the academies to provide conferences, seminars, nondistrict and district requested technical assistance, and production of instructionally related materials.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.71.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/398/#laws.3.31.0">1987 c 398 art 3 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.12.2.0">1989 c 329 art 12 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.11.5.0">1992 c 499 art 11 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.138.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 138</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.32.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.12.11.0">2023 c 55 art 12 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.72">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.72 STUDENT ACTIVITIES ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.72.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Student activities; receipts; appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>All receipts of any kind generated to operate student activities, including student fees, donations and contributions, and gate receipts must be deposited in the state treasury. The receipts are appropriated annually to the Minnesota State Academies for student activities purposes. They are not subject to budgetary control by the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.72.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">To student activities account.</span></h2>
          <p>The money appropriated in subdivision 1 to the Minnesota State Academies for student activities must be credited to a Minnesota State Academies' student activities account and may be spent only for Minnesota State Academies' student activities purposes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.72.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Carryover.</span></h2>
          <p>An unexpended balance in the Minnesota State Academies' student activities account may be carried over from the first fiscal year of the biennium into the second fiscal year of the biennium and from one biennium to the next. The amount carried over must not be taken into account in determining state appropriations and must not be deducted from a later appropriation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.72.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Money from certain student activities specifically included among receipts.</span></h2>
          <p>Any money generated by a Minnesota State Academies' student activity that involves:</p>
          <p>(1) state employees who are receiving compensation for their involvement with the activity;</p>
          <p>(2) the use of state facilities; or</p>
          <p>(3) money raised for student activities in the name of the Minnesota State Academies</p>
          <p class="bl">is specifically included among the kinds of receipts that are described in subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.72.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.11.5.0">1993 c 224 art 11 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.33.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.73">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.73 DUTIES OF STATE DEPARTMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.73.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Department of Education.</span></h2>
          <p>The Department of Education must assist the board of the Minnesota State Academies in preparing reports on the academies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.73.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Department of Management and Budget.</span></h2>
          <p>The Department of Management and Budget, in cooperation with the board of the Minnesota State Academies, must develop a statement of necessary qualifications and skills for all staff members of the academies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.73.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/220/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 220 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.23.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 23</a>,24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.34.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.42.0">2008 c 204 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SPECIAL NEEDS FUNDING</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.74">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.74 MEDICAL ASSISTANCE PAYMENTS TO SCHOOL DISTRICTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.74.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may enroll as a provider in the medical assistance program and receive medical assistance payments for covered evaluations and special education services provided to persons eligible for medical assistance under chapter 256B. To receive medical assistance payments, the district must pay the nonfederal share of medical assistance services provided according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0625#stat.256B.0625.26">256B.0625, subdivision 26</a>, and comply with relevant provisions of state and federal statutes and regulations governing the medical assistance program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.74.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Funding.</span></h2>
          <p>A district that provides a covered service to an eligible person and complies with relevant requirements of the medical assistance program is entitled to receive payment for the portion of the services that will subsequently be reimbursed by the federal government, in the same manner as other medical assistance providers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.74.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contract for services.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may contract for the provision of medical assistance-covered services, and may contract with a third-party agency to assist in administering and billing for these services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.74.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Private insurers.</span></h2>
          <p>A district may enroll as a provider for insurance companies to provide covered special education services to eligible persons. To receive payments, the district must comply with relevant state and federal statutes. A district may contract for services, and may contract with a third-party agency to assist in administering and billing for these services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.74.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">No reduction in revenue.</span></h2>
          <p>A district's revenue for special education programs must not be reduced by any payments for medical assistance or insurance received according to this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.74.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/191/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 191 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/562/#laws.3.6.0">1990 c 562 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/647/#laws.3.16.0">1994 c 647 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.121.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 121</a>,164; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/488/#laws.11.2.0">2000 c 488 art 11 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.4.6.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 4 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.744">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.744 STATEWIDE DATA MANAGEMENT SYSTEM TO MAXIMIZE MEDICAL ASSISTANCE REIMBURSEMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.744.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, cooperative unit has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.744.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Statewide data management system.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of education, in cooperation with the commissioner of human services, shall develop a statewide data management system using the educational data reporting system or other existing data management system for school districts and cooperative units to use to maximize medical assistance reimbursement for health and health-related services provided under individualized education programs and individual family service plans. The system must be appropriately integrated with state and local existing and developing human services and education data systems. The statewide data management system must enable school district and cooperative unit staff to:</p>
          <p>(1) establish medical assistance billing systems or improve existing systems;</p>
          <p>(2) understand the appropriate medical assistance billing codes for services provided under individualized education programs and individual family service plans;</p>
          <p>(3) comply with the Individuals with Disabilities Education Act, Public Law 105-17;</p>
          <p>(4) contract with billing agents; and</p>
          <p>(5) carry out other activities necessary to maximize medical assistance reimbursement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.744.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Implementation.</span></h2>
          <p>Consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0625#stat.256B.0625.26">256B.0625, subdivision 26</a>, school districts may enroll as medical assistance providers or subcontractors and bill the Department of Human Services under the medical assistance fee for service claims processing system for special education services which are covered services under chapter 256B, which are provided in the school setting for a medical assistance recipient, and for whom the district has secured informed consent consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.05#stat.13.05.4">13.05, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (d), and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.77#stat.256B.77.2">256B.77, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (p), to bill for each type of covered service. School districts shall be reimbursed by the commissioner of human services for the federal share of individualized education program health-related services that qualify for reimbursement by medical assistance, minus up to five percent retained by the commissioner of human services for administrative costs, not to exceed $450,000 per fiscal year. The commissioner may withhold up to five percent of each payment to a school district. Following the end of each fiscal year, the commissioner shall settle up with each school district in order to ensure that collections from each district for departmental administrative costs are made on a pro rata basis according to federal earnings for these services in each district. A school district is not eligible to enroll as a home care provider or a personal care provider organization for purposes of billing home care services under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0653">256B.0653</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654</a> until the commissioner of human services issues a bulletin instructing county public health nurses on how to assess for the needs of eligible recipients during school hours. To use home care nursing services or personal care services at school, the recipient or responsible party must provide written authorization in the care plan identifying the chosen provider and the daily amount of services to be used at school.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.744.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.2.2.0">1998 c 398 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/245/#laws.4.4.0">1999 c 245 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.3.18.0">2009 c 96 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.3.12.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.4.9.0">2014 c 262 art 4 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/291/#laws.9.5.0">2014 c 291 art 9 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.75">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.75 SPECIAL EDUCATION PROGRAMS; APPROVAL; AID PAYMENTS; TRAVEL AID; LITIGATION COSTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Travel aid.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The state must pay each district one-half of the sum actually expended by a district, based on mileage, for necessary travel of essential personnel providing home-based or community-based services to children with a disability under age five and their families.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Aid payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The aids provided for children with a disability must be paid to the district providing the special instruction and services. General education aid must be paid to the district of the pupil's residence. The total amount of aid paid may not exceed the amount expended for children with a disability in the year for which the aid is paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Full state payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The state must pay each district the actual cost incurred in providing instruction and services for a child whose district of residence has been determined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.17">125A.17</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.51">125A.51, paragraph (b)</a>, and who is temporarily placed in a state institution, a licensed residential facility, or foster facility for care and treatment. The regular education program at the facility must be an approved program according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.515">125A.515</a>.</p>
          <p>Upon following the procedure specified by the commissioner, the district may bill the state the actual cost incurred in providing the services including transportation costs and a proportionate amount of capital expenditures and debt service, minus the amount of the basic revenue, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.2">126C.10, subdivision 2</a>, of the district for the child and the special education aid, transportation aid, and any other aid earned on behalf of the child. The limit in subdivision 2 applies to aid paid pursuant to this subdivision.</p>
          <p>To the extent possible, the commissioner shall obtain reimbursement from another state for the cost of serving any child whose parent or guardian resides in that state. The commissioner may contract with the appropriate authorities of other states to effect reimbursement. All money received from other states must be paid to the state treasury and placed in the general fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Program and aid approval.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Before June 1 of each year, each district providing special instruction and services to children with a disability, including children eligible for Part C, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.02#stat.125A.02.1">125A.02, subdivision 1</a>, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.27">125A.27</a>, subdivision 8, must submit to the commissioner an application for approval of these programs and their budgets for the next fiscal year. The application must include an enumeration of the costs proposed as eligible for state aid pursuant to this section and of the estimated number and grade level of children with a disability in the district who will receive special instruction and services during the next fiscal year. The application must also include any other information deemed necessary by the commissioner for the calculation of state aid and for the evaluation of the necessity of the program, the necessity of the personnel to be employed in the program, for determining the amount which the program will receive from grants from federal funds, or special grants from other state sources, and the program's compliance with the rules and standards of the Department of Education. The commissioner shall review each application to determine whether the program and the personnel to be employed in the program are actually necessary and essential to meet the district's obligation to provide special instruction and services to children with a disability pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.259">125A.259</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.48">125A.48</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>. The commissioner shall not approve aid pursuant to this section for any program or for the salary of any personnel determined to be unnecessary or unessential on the basis of this review. The commissioner may withhold all or any portion of the aid for programs which receive grants from federal funds, or special grants from other state sources. By August 31 the commissioner shall approve, disapprove, or modify each application, and notify each applying district of the action and of the estimated amount of aid for the programs. The commissioner shall provide procedures for districts to submit additional applications for program and budget approval during the fiscal year, for programs needed to meet any substantial changes in the needs of children with a disability in the district. Notwithstanding the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.42">127A.42</a>, the commissioner may modify or withdraw the program or aid approval and withhold aid pursuant to this section without proceeding according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.42">127A.42</a> at any time the commissioner determines that the program does not comply with rules of the Department of Education or that any facts concerning the program or its budget differ from the facts in the district's approved application.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Regular classroom programs.</span></h2>
          <p>When planning programs for the education of children with a disability in the regular classroom, school districts are encouraged to consider the size of the regular class and to provide the support services necessary to ensure successful mainstreaming.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.3.25.0">2007 c 146 art 3 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.4.13.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 4 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.3.19.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 3 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.75.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.1.26.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 1 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.76">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.76 SPECIAL EDUCATION AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For the purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>, the definitions in this subdivision apply.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) "Basic revenue" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.2">126C.10, subdivision 2</a>. For the purposes of computing basic revenue pursuant to this section, each child with a disability shall be counted as prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05#stat.126C.05.1">126C.05, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) "Essential personnel" means teachers, cultural liaisons, related services, and support services staff providing services to students. Essential personnel may also include special education paraprofessionals or clericals providing support to teachers and students by preparing paperwork and making arrangements related to special education compliance requirements, including parent meetings and individualized education programs. Essential personnel does not include administrators and supervisors.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) "Average daily membership" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.05">126C.05</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) "Program growth factor" means 1.046 for fiscal year 2017, and the product of 1.046 and the program growth factor for the previous year for fiscal year 2018 and later.</p>
          <p>(f) "Nonfederal special education expenditure" means all direct expenditures that are necessary and essential to meet the district's obligation to provide special instruction and services to children with a disability according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.454">124D.454</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.259">125A.259</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.48">125A.48</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a> as submitted by the district and approved by the department under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.75#stat.125A.75.4">125A.75, subdivision 4</a>, excluding expenditures:</p>
          <p>(1) reimbursed with federal funds;</p>
          <p>(2) reimbursed with other state aids under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(3) for general education costs of serving students with a disability;</p>
          <p>(4) for facilities;</p>
          <p>(5) for pupil transportation; and</p>
          <p>(6) for postemployment benefits.</p>
          <p>(g) "Old formula special education expenditures" means expenditures eligible for revenue under Minnesota Statutes 2012, section 125A.76, subdivision 2.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) For the Minnesota State Academy for the Deaf and the Minnesota State Academy for the Blind, expenditures under paragraphs (f) and (g) are limited to the salary and fringe benefits of one-to-one instructional and behavior management aides and one-to-one licensed, certified professionals assigned to a child attending the academy, if the aides or professionals are required by the child's individualized education program.</p>
          <p>(i) "Special education aid increase limit" means $80 for fiscal year 2016, $100 for fiscal year 2017, and, for fiscal years 2018 through 2020, the sum of the special education aid increase limit for the previous fiscal year and $40.</p>
          <p>(j) "District" means a school district, a charter school, or a cooperative unit as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>. Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.26">123A.26</a>, cooperative units as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>, are eligible to receive special education aid under this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>.</p>
          <p>(k) "Initial special education cross subsidy" means the greater of zero or:</p>
          <p>(1) the nonfederal special education expenditure under paragraph (f); plus</p>
          <p>(2) the cost of providing transportation services for pupils with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4); minus</p>
          <p>(3) the special education aid under subdivision 2c and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11#stat.125A.11.1">125A.11, subdivision 1</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>; minus</p>
          <p>(4) the amount of general education revenue, excluding local optional revenue, plus local optional aid and referendum equalization aid attributable to pupils receiving special instruction and services outside the regular classroom for more than 60 percent of the school day for the portion of time the pupils receive special instruction and services outside the regular classroom, excluding portions attributable to district and school administration, district support services, operations and maintenance, capital expenditures, and pupil transportation.</p>
          <p>(l) The "minimum aid adjustment multiplier" for fiscal year 2020 equals <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.046">1.046</a>. For fiscal year 2021 and later, the minimum aid adjustment multiplier equals the greater of <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.02">1.02</a> or the minimum aid adjustment multiplier for the previous year minus 0.002.</p>
          <p>(m) The "minimum aid adjustment factor" for fiscal year 2020 equals the program growth factor for fiscal year 2020. For fiscal year 2021 and later, the minimum aid adjustment factor equals the product of the minimum aid adjustment factor for the previous fiscal year and the minimum aid adjustment multiplier.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Special education initial aid.</span></h2>
          <p>For fiscal year 2021 and later, a district's special education initial aid equals the sum of:</p>
          <p>(1) the least of 62 percent of the district's old formula special education expenditures for the prior fiscal year, excluding pupil transportation expenditures, 50 percent of the district's nonfederal special education expenditures for the prior year, excluding pupil transportation expenditures, or 56 percent of the product of the sum of the following amounts, computed using prior fiscal year data, and the program growth factor:</p>
          <p>(i) the product of the district's average daily membership served and the sum of:</p>
          <p>(A) $460; plus</p>
          <p>(B) $405 times the ratio of the sum of the number of pupils enrolled on October 1 who are eligible to receive free meals plus one-half of the pupils enrolled on October 1 who are eligible to receive reduced-price meals to the total October 1 enrollment; plus</p>
          <p>(C) .008 times the district's average daily membership served; plus</p>
          <p>(ii) $13,300 times the December 1 child count for the primary disability areas of autism spectrum disorders, developmental delay, and severely multiply impaired; plus</p>
          <p>(iii) $19,200 times the December 1 child count for the primary disability areas of deaf and hard-of-hearing and emotional or behavioral disorders; plus</p>
          <p>(iv) $25,200 times the December 1 child count for the primary disability areas of developmentally cognitive mild-moderate, developmentally cognitive severe-profound, physically impaired, visually impaired, and deafblind; plus</p>
          <p>(2) the cost of providing transportation services for children with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.4.13.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 4 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.<span class="headnote">Special education aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For fiscal year 2020 and later, a district's special education aid equals the sum of the district's special education initial aid under subdivision 2a, the district's cross subsidy reduction aid under subdivision 2e, special education homeless pupil aid under subdivision 2f, and the district's excess cost aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79#stat.125A.79.5">125A.79, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), for fiscal year 2020, the special education aid for a school district, excluding the cross subsidy reduction aid under subdivision 2e, and excluding special education homeless pupil aid under subdivision 2f, must not exceed the greater of:</p>
          <p>(i) the sum of 56 percent of the district's nonfederal special education expenditures plus 100 percent of the district's cost of providing transportation services for children with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4), plus the adjustment under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11">125A.11</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the sum of: (A) the product of the district's average daily membership served and the special education aid increase limit; and (B) the product of the sum of the special education aid the district would have received for fiscal year 2016 under Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>, as adjusted according to Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11">125A.11</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, the ratio of the district's average daily membership served for the current fiscal year to the district's average daily membership served for fiscal year 2016, and the program growth factor.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), for fiscal year 2024 and later the special education aid, excluding the cross subsidy reduction aid under subdivision 2e, excluding special education homeless pupil aid under subdivision 2f, for a school district, not including a charter school or cooperative unit as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24">123A.24</a>, must not be less than the lesser of (1) the sum of 90 percent for fiscal year 2020, 85 percent for fiscal year 2021, 80 percent for fiscal year 2022, and 75 percent for fiscal year 2023 and later of the district's nonfederal special education expenditures plus 100 percent of the district's cost of providing transportation services for children with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4), plus the adjustment under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11">125A.11 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, for that fiscal year, or (2) the product of the sum of the special education aid the district would have received for fiscal year 2016 under Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>, as adjusted according to Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11">125A.11 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, the ratio of the district's adjusted daily membership for the current fiscal year to the district's average daily membership for fiscal year 2016, and the minimum aid adjustment factor.</p>
          <p>(d) Notwithstanding subdivision 2a and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>, a charter school in its first year of operation shall generate special education aid based on current year data. A newly formed cooperative unit as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24">123A.24</a> may apply to the commissioner for approval to generate special education aid for its first year of operation based on current year data, with an offsetting adjustment to the prior year data used to calculate aid for programs at participating school districts or previous cooperatives that were replaced by the new cooperative. The department shall establish procedures to adjust the prior year data and fiscal year 2016 old formula aid used in calculating special education aid to exclude costs that have been eliminated for districts where programs have closed or where a substantial portion of the program has been transferred to a cooperative unit.</p>
          <p>(e) The department shall establish procedures through the uniform financial accounting and reporting system to identify and track all revenues generated from third-party billings as special education revenue at the school district level; include revenue generated from third-party billings as special education revenue in the annual cross-subsidy report; and exclude third-party revenue from calculation of excess cost aid to the districts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2d.<span class="headnote">Statewide average expenditure.</span></h2>
          <p>By January 15 of each year, the department must calculate the statewide average special education expenditure per December 1 child count for the prior fiscal year by primary disability area and provide that information to all districts. By January 15 of each odd-numbered year, the commissioner must identify options for aligning the assignment of disability areas to the categories and the rates for each category in subdivision 2a, clause (1), with the latest expenditure data and submit these options to the legislative committees with jurisdiction over education finance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2e.<span class="headnote">Cross subsidy reduction aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A school district's annual cross subsidy reduction aid equals the school district's initial special education cross subsidy for the previous fiscal year times the cross subsidy aid factor for that fiscal year.</p>
          <p>(b) The cross subsidy aid factor equals 6.43 percent for fiscal year 2023; 44 percent for fiscal years 2024, 2025, and 2026; and 50 percent for fiscal year 2027 and later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.2f">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2f.<span class="headnote">Special education homeless pupil aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For fiscal year 2024 and later, special education homeless pupil aid must be paid to a school district that is funded for that year based on the district's fiscal year 2016 expenditures calculated under Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79">125A.79</a>, as adjusted according to Minnesota Statutes 2012, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.11">125A.11</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, the ratio of the district's adjusted daily membership for the current fiscal year to the district's average daily membership for fiscal year 2016, and the minimum aid adjustment factor.</p>
          <p>(b) Special education homeless pupil aid equals the greater of zero, or a district's prior year transportation costs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4), items (ii) and (vii), and the additional cost of transporting a student in a shelter care facility as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.007#stat.260C.007.30">260C.007, subdivision 30</a>, a homeless student in another district to the school of origin, or a formerly homeless student from a permanent home in another district to the school of origin but only through the end of the academic year; minus the fiscal year 2016 costs associated with transportation costs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.92#stat.123B.92.1">123B.92, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clause (4), items (ii) and (vii), and the additional cost of transporting a student in a shelter care facility as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.007#stat.260C.007.30">260C.007, subdivision 30</a>, a homeless student in another district to the school of origin, or a formerly homeless student from a permanent home in another district to the school of origin, but only through the end of the academic year; adjusted by the ratio of the district's adjusted daily membership for the current fiscal year to the district's average daily membership for fiscal year 2016, and the minimum aid adjustment factor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.3.25.0">2007 c 146 art 3 s 25</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Adjustments for tuition reciprocity with adjoining states.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If an agreement is reached between the state of Minnesota and an adjoining state pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.041">124D.041</a> that requires a special education tuition payment from the state of Minnesota to the adjoining state, the tuition payment shall be made from the special education aid appropriation for that year.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If an agreement is reached between the state of Minnesota and an adjoining state pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.041">124D.041</a> that requires special education tuition payments to be made between the two states and not between districts in the two states, the special education aid for a Minnesota school district serving a student with a disability from the adjoining state shall be calculated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.47#stat.127A.47.7">127A.47, subdivision 7</a>, except that no reduction shall be made in the special education aid paid to the resident district.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.62.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 62</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Special education forecast maintenance of effort.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If, on the basis of a forecast of general fund revenues and expenditures under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.103">16A.103</a>, the state's expenditures for special education and related services for children with disabilities from nonfederal sources for a fiscal year, including special education aid under subdivision 2c; travel for home-based services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.75#stat.125A.75.1">125A.75, subdivision 1</a>; aid for students with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.75#stat.125A.75.3">125A.75, subdivision 3</a>; court-placed special education under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79#stat.125A.79.4">125A.79, subdivision 4</a>; out-of-state tuition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79#stat.125A.79.8">125A.79, subdivision 8</a>; and direct expenditures by state agencies are projected to be less than the amount required to meet federal special education maintenance of effort, the reimbursement percentages for excess cost aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.79#stat.125A.79.5">125A.79, subdivision 5</a>, must be increased as required to ensure that the additional amount required to meet federal special education maintenance of effort is added to the state total special education aid in subdivision 2c.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If, on the basis of a forecast of general fund revenues and expenditures under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.103">16A.103</a>, expenditures in the programs in paragraph (a) are projected to be greater than previously forecast for an enacted budget, and an addition to state total special education aid has been made under paragraph (a), the state total special education aid must be reduced by the lesser of the amount of the expenditure increase or the amount previously added to state total special education aid in subdivision 2c.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For the purpose of this section, "previously forecast for an enacted budget" means the allocation of funding for these programs in the most recent forecast of general fund revenues and expenditures or the act appropriating money for these programs, whichever occurred most recently. It does not include planning estimates for a future biennium.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) If the amount of special education aid is adjusted in accordance with this subdivision, the commissioner of education shall notify the chairs of the legislative committees having jurisdiction over kindergarten through grade 12 education regarding the amount of the adjustment and provide an explanation of the federal maintenance of effort requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.76.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Funding.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner and the commissioner of children, youth, and families shall enter into an agreement, upon federal approval, under which the commissioner of education shall distribute funds appropriated for programs under this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.76.history
			">

        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.15.10.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 15 s 10</a>; art 16 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/412/#laws.3.19.0">1996 c 412 art 3 s 19</a>-24;       <div class="sr" id="stat.125A.77"><b>125A.77</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.62.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 62</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.78">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.78 ALTERNATIVE DELIVERY AID ADJUSTMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.78.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A district is eligible for an alternative delivery aid adjustment if the commissioner has approved the application of the district according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.50">125A.50</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.78.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote"> Aid adjustment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For the fiscal year after approval of a district's application, and thereafter, the special education aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a> must be computed based on activities defined as reimbursable under Department of Education rules for special education and nonspecial education students, and additional activities as detailed and approved by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.78.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Use of revenue.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a> shall be used to implement the approved program.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.78.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/265/#laws.3.10.0">1991 c 265 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.3.19.0">1993 c 224 art 3 s 19</a>-22; art 14 s 11,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.15.14.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 15 s 14</a>; art 16 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/7/#laws.1.59.0">1997 c 7 art 1 s 59</a>,60; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.2.104.0">1998 c 397 art 2 s 104</a>,105,164; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.55.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.3.16.0">2007 c 146 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.24.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.1.20.0">2014 c 275 art 1 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.79">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.79 SPECIAL EDUCATION EXCESS COST AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For the purposes of this section, the definitions in this subdivision apply.</p>
          <p class="in">(a) "Unreimbursed old formula special education expenditures" means:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) old formula special education expenditures for the prior fiscal year; minus</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for fiscal year 2016 and later, the special education initial aid under section 125A.76, subdivision 2a; minus</p>
          <p>(3) for fiscal year 2016 and later, the amount of general education revenue, excluding local optional revenue, plus local optional aid and referendum equalization aid for the prior fiscal year attributable to pupils receiving special instruction and services outside the regular classroom for more than 60 percent of the school day for the portion of time the pupils receive special instruction and services outside the regular classroom, excluding portions attributable to district and school administration, district support services, operations and maintenance, capital expenditures, and pupil transportation.</p>
          <p>(b) "Unreimbursed nonfederal special education expenditures" means:</p>
          <p>(1) nonfederal special education expenditures for the prior fiscal year; minus</p>
          <p>(2) special education initial aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76#stat.125A.76.2a">125A.76, subdivision 2a</a>; minus</p>
          <p>(3) the amount of general education revenue, excluding local optional revenue, plus local optional aid, and referendum equalization aid for the prior fiscal year attributable to pupils receiving special instruction and services outside the regular classroom for more than 60 percent of the school day for the portion of time the pupils receive special instruction and services outside of the regular classroom, excluding portions attributable to district and school administration, district support services, operations and maintenance, capital expenditures, and pupil transportation.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) "General revenue" for a school district means the sum of the general education revenue according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/126C.10#stat.126C.10.1">126C.10, subdivision 1</a>, excluding transportation sparsity revenue, local optional revenue, and total operating capital revenue. "General revenue" for a charter school means the sum of the general education revenue according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124E.20#stat.124E.20.1">124E.20, subdivision 1</a>, and transportation revenue according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124E.23">124E.23</a>, excluding referendum equalization aid, transportation sparsity revenue, and operating capital revenue.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.3.21.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 3 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.2.62.0">1999 c 241 art 2 s 62</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Tuition.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.03">125A.03</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.24">125A.24</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.65">125A.65</a>, for children who are nonresidents of Minnesota, receive services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a>, subdivisions 1 and 2a, and are placed in the serving school district by court action, the serving school district shall submit unreimbursed tuition bills for eligible services to the Department of Education instead of the resident school district. To be eligible for reimbursement, the serving school district, as part of its child intake procedures, must demonstrate good faith effort to obtain from the placing agency a financial commitment to pay tuition costs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote"> Excess cost aid.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For fiscal year 2016 and later, a district's excess cost aid equals the greater of:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 56 percent of the difference between (i) the district's unreimbursed nonfederal special education expenditures and (ii) 7.0 percent of the product of the ratio of $5,831 to the formula allowance for the prior year and the district's general revenue;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 62 percent of the difference between (i) the district's unreimbursed old formula special education expenditures and (ii) 2.5 percent of the product of the ratio of $5,831 to the formula allowance for the prior year and the district's general revenue; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) zero.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.5.32.0">2013 c 116 art 5 s 32</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.79.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Out-of-state tuition.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For children who are residents of the state, receive services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a>, subdivisions 1 and 2a, and are placed in a care and treatment facility by court action in a state that does not have a reciprocity agreement with the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.155">125A.155</a>, the resident school district shall receive special education out-of-state tuition aid equal to the amount of the tuition bills, minus (1) the general education revenue, excluding basic skills revenue and the local optional levy attributable to the pupil, calculated using the resident district's average general education revenue per adjusted pupil unit, (2) the referendum equalization aid attributable to the pupil, calculated using the resident district's referendum equalization aid per adjusted pupil unit, and (3) the special education aid attributable to the pupil.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.125A.80"><b>125A.80</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.3.6.0">2012 c 239 art 3 s 6</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.125A.81">
        <h1 class="shn">125A.81 SPECIAL EDUCATION SEPARATE SITES AND PROGRAMS AID.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.81.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "special education separate site and program" means a public separate day school facility attended by students with disabilities for 50 percent or more of their school day.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.81.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligibility for special education separate sites and programs aid.</span></h2>
          <p>An education cooperative under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/471.59">471.59</a>, education district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.15">123A.15</a>, service cooperative under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.21">123A.21</a>, or intermediate school district under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136D.01">136D.01</a> qualifies for additional state funding to special education separate sites and programs for every kindergarten through grade 12 child with a disability, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.02">125A.02</a>, served in a special education separate site or program as defined in subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.81.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Uses of special education separate sites and programs aid.</span></h2>
          <p>Additional state funding to special education separate sites and programs under this section may be used for the same purposes as are permitted for state special education aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.76">125A.76</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.125A.81.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Special education separate sites and programs aid.</span></h2>
          <p>For fiscal year 2024 and later, additional state funding to special education separate sites and programs equals $1,689 times the adjusted kindergarten through grade 12 pupil units served in special education separate sites and programs under subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.125A.81.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.7.15.0">2023 c 55 art 7 s 15</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
